Not done: the sample is the first record only — a field that is empty in the first product and filled in the rest shows nothing, and a "sample from any record" needs the scan to keep more than one item; type labels are a table beside the model, not derived from the catalogue's parsers; the table's required groups are declared in `ImportMappingTable` to match `XmlCatalog.RequiredFields` and a test pins the labels, but not the two lists against each other at runtime; the recompute after a cell edit is deferred to background priority and the combo's own edit commit may lag it by one keystroke; DPI is asserted through fixed DIP column widths, not a rendered 200 % test.

## #825 — import validation result filters (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-825-import-validation-filters off codex/issue-824-import-mapping-readability

New issue. DESIGN: the XML preview grid showed every row with no way to narrow it to the ones that matter — the rows a rule complains about, the rows that touch a given field, the rows that would actually change something in the pool — and no count that said how many of each there were.

`ImportValidationFilter.cs` (new, pure): `Evaluate(rows, existingFor)` runs the catalogue's own rules (`ProductValidation.Evaluate`) over every preview row **once** and reduces each to flags — highest level, the fields that fired, stable reason codes (`section:field`, ASCII-folded, so the same rule always files under the same code), and a change classification against the pool (`New`, `Changed`, `Unchanged`, comparing what the import would write: name, price, cost, stock, currency, description, brand, category, images). `Apply(rows, state)` combines severity AND field AND reason code AND changed-only in one pass over the flags, and returns the matching indices with **counts that describe the filtered set** — total, matching, blocking, warning, clean, changed — plus the option lists. Zero results with rows present is a state (`IsEmptyResult`), not an error. Filter options are field names and codes derived from the rules; a supplier's product name, price or address is never a label.

Wired: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — after `PreviewAsync` computes rows, `EvaluatePreviewRows` builds the flags (pool lookup by SKU, then barcode) and fills the filter bar; a "Doğrulama" column shows each row's status and change ("✔ temiz · yeni", "⚠ Uyarı · değişen") through a converter keyed by row reference, so the grid's items stay `CatalogProduct` and the import's selection path is untouched; the bar above the grid holds Önem / Alan / Neden combos, a "Yalnız değişenler" check box and a count line ("2 / 3 satır · 0 engel · 1 uyarı · 2 değişen", with "filtre hiçbir satırla eşleşmiyor" when nothing matches); every control re-applies the filter, and "Temiz" is the rows with no finding.

**Fact learned and kept honest:** `XmlCatalog.Preview` pre-validates every blocking rule — a row with no name or identity, a negative number, a duplicate SKU or barcode makes the preview *throw* — so a previewed XML row can never carry a blocking finding. On this grid the severity dimension is warnings (missing description or images, low stock, …), and "Engel" is the zero-result case. The model still supports blocking rows for grids that can hold them.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportValidationFilterTests.cs` (new, 5, red first): severity, field, reason-code and changed-only filters combining with counts that follow the filter; zero results as a state with the total still known; change classification on name, price and stock; filter options never carrying a row value and codes being stable ASCII; a hundred thousand rows evaluated once and filtered twice in a pass over flags. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportValidationFilterWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): the real XML page reading a three-record file against a pool that holds two of them — "3 / 3" before filtering, the warning filter narrowing to the description-less row, changed-only dropping the row identical to the pool, warning-and-changed combining to one, "Engel" giving the spoken zero-result state, keyboard-reachable controls, and no row value in any label.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=1; FILES_CHANGED=ImportValidationFilter.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportValidationFilterTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportValidationFilterWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=480 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (full Release suite, up from 474); Release build and self-contained win-x64 publish succeeded; artifact SHA-256 57753b7aad27d3a38e582ec2a5b7a240e4510a69a7ac4086dd4cf2fd4d47b00a.
Not done: the filter bar is on the XML preview only — the Excel import preview has its own error list and no filter yet; a filtered "select all" followed by import is a partial import by design (the feed hash is only recorded for a complete selection), but the count line does not yet say "seçim kısmi olacak"; the change classification compares the fields the import writes, not the pool's version stamp, so an unrelated pool edit reads as unchanged; the 100k evidence is a pure-model timing bound, not a rendered grid of that size.

## #826 — import rejected-rows export (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-826-rejected-rows-export off codex/issue-825-import-validation-filters

New issue. DESIGN: the only rejected-rows export in the app ("Hataları Excel'e aktar" on the Excel screen) wrote one column of raw sentences — no row id, no reason code, no field, nothing a script could fix against — with no redaction, no cancellation and no result on a disk error. And, as the real-path test showed, it had never worked: see the finding below.

`RejectedRowsExport.cs` (new): a **stable five-column schema** (`satir`, `neden_kodu`, `alan`, `guvenli_deger`, `mesaj`; `SchemaVersion` 1) that a fix-and-retry loop can be scripted against. `Parse` lifts row number, field and reason code out of the import's own sentences ("Satır 3: Stock negatif olamaz. (NEGATIVE)" → 3 / NEGATIVE / Stock) and never invents what it did not find (no code → `UNKNOWN`, no known field leading the sentence → empty, no row → 0 and sorted last). `From` is for callers that have the raw value, which is made safe here: through the uncapped redaction, a raw body replaced, one short line. `Build` returns only the refused rows, in row order. `WriteCsv` is UTF-8 with BOM (so Excel opens it), RFC 4180 quoting, written beside the target and moved at the end; it checks cancellation per row and returns a **result** — success with the count, cancelled, or a disk error with a redacted message — and never leaves a partial or temp file.

Wired: `Catalog/CatalogExcel.cs` — `ExportErrors(path, preview, token)` now writes the schema to the xlsx through the same `Build`, cancellable and atomic, returning the same result type; the old one-argument overload delegates to it. `MainWindow.Excel.cs` — the button became "Reddedilen satırları dışa aktar", saves as xlsx or CSV by extension, runs the export off the UI thread with a visible "Dışa aktarımı iptal et" button while it writes, and reports success ("N reddedilen satır dışa aktarıldı (şema v1)"), cancellation, or a disk error through the error toast rather than a crash.

**Defect found and fixed on the way:** every `CatalogExcel` writer saved to `path + ".tmp-<guid>"`, and ClosedXML refuses a file name without an `.xlsx` extension ("Extension 'tmp-…' is not supported") — so the **product export and the old error export threw on every click**, and nothing had tested them end to end. The temp name now keeps `.xlsx` in all writers, and `CatalogExcelExportTests` pins the product export by reading the file back.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/RejectedRowsExportTests.cs` (new, 5, red first): the schema pinned and the import's sentences parsed with nothing invented; none, one (with quoting) and a hundred thousand rows written; a cancellation leaving no file and reported as a result; a disk error (a directory as the target) reported with no temp file left and an empty path refused; values and messages redacted, a body replaced. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/RejectedRowsXlsxExportTests.cs` (new, 3, through `CatalogExcel.ExportErrors`): the xlsx header equal to the schema and rows in row order read back with ClosedXML; a cancelled export leaving nothing; a disk error as a result. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CatalogExcelExportTests.cs` (new, 1): the product export writing a readable file with no temp leftover.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=RejectedRowsExport.cs (new), Catalog/CatalogExcel.cs, MainWindow.Excel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/RejectedRowsExportTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/RejectedRowsXlsxExportTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/CatalogExcelExportTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=489 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (full Release suite, up from 480); Release build and self-contained win-x64 publish succeeded; artifact SHA-256 6e06375cc517565734d70139838f481f947e81b65f37f9207fb91dcc296ae5bf.
Not done: the XML import has no rejected-rows list to export — its preview throws on the first refused row rather than collecting them, so an "export the refused XML rows" needs the preview to collect first; the parsed sentences carry no raw value (the Excel path's messages never include one), so `guvenli_deger` is empty for them and only filled by callers that use `From`; the reason code is whatever the sentence carries in parentheses and `UNKNOWN` otherwise — a code table for the sentences without one is the import's own change; the cancel button is exercised through the writer's token in tests, not by clicking it in a window test.

## #827 — Import progress stage breakdown (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-827-import-progress-stages off codex/issue-826-rejected-rows-export (a25ba00)

New issue. DESIGN: the XML import ran as one opaque wait — no stage, no count, no way to tell a slow download from a slow apply, and no cancel while it ran. Nothing in the pipeline reported progress.

`ImportProgress.cs` (new): six stages in pipeline order (`Download`, `Read`, `Parse`, `Validate`, `Preview`, `Apply` labelled "Havuza aktar (yerel)"), each fed only by real `ImportProgressEvent`s — a stage starts, counts `Done` against a `Total` when one is known, and ends done, failed or cancelled. `ImportStageProgress.Percent` exists only with a positive total; otherwise the bar is indeterminate with a count, never an estimate. Elapsed is measured while running; an 80 ms parse is recorded like a 60 s preview. `Cancel(now)` lands on the running stage and leaves later stages pending; `Retry()` resets the failed/cancelled stage and everything after it, keeping what finished; starting an earlier stage resets later ones (a re-read invalidates preview and apply). Notes pass through `AuditStore.Redact`, so a stage can say "12 ürün" but never quote the source address or a credential.

Wired into the real pipeline: `XmlCatalog.Preview(xml, source, IProgress?)` reports every 500 items (count only — the total is unknown until enumeration ends); `CatalogStore.Import(..., IProgress?)`/`ImportCore` report every 200 rows and at the end against the real row count. `MainWindow.xaml.cs`: `importProgress` state + `importProgressPanel` under the stepper (one row per stage: glyph, label, `ProgressBar` with `IsIndeterminate` when the total is unknown, counter · status word · elapsed · note, `AutomationProperties.Name` carrying the same); the `Progress<T>` reporter is created on the UI thread so background stages marshal; `InspectAsync` reports Download (done = characters read) and Read (done = fields found), `PreviewAsync` reports Parse (total = item count from `MappingSnapshot`), Validate (`EnsureMappingReady`) and Preview (with the sink), `ImportAsync` reports Apply with the sink and "N yeni / M güncel" when done; each stage catches `OperationCanceledException` → Cancelled and any other exception → Failed with `Safe(e)` and rethrows so the existing error paths still run. `importCts` is linked to `lifetime` per stage; the "İptal" button (visible only while a stage runs) cancels it and marks the stage; "Yeniden dene" (visible only after a failure/cancel) re-runs the failed stage's method (Download/Read → `InspectAsync`, Apply → `ImportAsync`, else `PreviewAsync`).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportProgressTests.cs` (new, 5, red first): known total → percentage, unknown → count + indeterminate bar + elapsed; fast and slow stages recorded alike and done fills the total; cancel lands on the running stage, keeps what finished, is a no-op with nothing running; retry resets from the failure and re-starting an earlier stage resets later ones; notes redacted, six stages ordered, apply says "yerel". `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportProgressWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real `MainWindow` on the XML page with a local three-item feed — `InspectAsync` + `PreviewAsync` drive the first five stages to Done from their own events (parse total 3, preview 3/3 = 100 %, download count > 0), Apply stays pending at 0 with no fake percentage, six bars none indeterminate, cancel hidden when nothing runs.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=ImportProgress.cs (new), Catalog/XmlCatalog.cs, Catalog/CatalogStore.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportProgressTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportProgressWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=495 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 489 + 6 new); artifact SHA-256 774c23f4214e1dd056c620e83057cd3078a8ae6ddabe5bae24e3411f1c85320e (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the Download stage has no byte total (`XmlSourceReader.ReadAsync` returns the whole string; Content-Length is not surfaced) so it is indeterminate until done; the Validate stage is `EnsureMappingReady` only — per-row `ProductValidation` runs inside Preview and is counted there; the scheduled/background import (`XmlSourceExecutionGate` runs from the scheduler) reports nothing to this panel — only the interactive path is wired; the window test does not exercise cancel mid-stage or retry against a live failure (the model tests cover both, the buttons are wired but only their visibility is asserted); the "Apply" retry re-runs the full import, not the failed rows only.

## #828 — Import completion summary (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-828-import-completion-summary off codex/issue-827-import-progress-stages (309bc37)

New issue. DESIGN: the XML apply ended in one status sentence ("N yeni / M güncel / K aynı") and an immediate jump to the product pool — no outcome, no duration, no revision, no reason on failure, no next step, and no distinction between "nothing changed" and "it worked".

`ImportCompletionSummary.cs` (new): `Compose(ImportCompletionInput, routeExists)` → `ImportCompletion` from the terminal result only — the store's `ImportSummary`, the error, or the cancellation. Five outcomes: Success; Partial (rows refused by a blocking finding while others applied); Failure and Cancelled (the transaction rolled back, so Applied = 0 and Rejected = the whole selection); NoOp (the store's `AlreadyApplied` for a complete feed already applied, or every selected row identical). Counts line (applied = added + updated, unchanged, rejected, warned), duration (`FormatDuration`: "2,5 sn" / "1 dk 5 sn", current culture), revision (source name · mapping revision · 8-char feed-hash prefix, or "kısmi seçim" when no complete feed hash was applied — never the address), detail (error through `AuditStore.Redact`, capped at 300; a payload or stack trace per `StatusTooltip.LooksLikeRawPayload` is replaced by a pointer to the diagnostics log), and next actions per outcome (Success → product pool [+ show warned rows]; Partial → show rejected, product pool; Failure → retry, diagnostics; Cancelled → retry; NoOp → re-read, product pool), route actions kept only when `routeExists`. Severity level per outcome drives `SeverityStyle` (glyph, accent, border weight; high-contrast aware).

Wired: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — `importCompletionPanel` under the progress panel (headline with glyph, counts, duration · revision, detail, action buttons; `AutomationProperties.Name` carries headline + counts), `CompleteImport(input)` composes, renders and raises a toast with the first routed action; `ImportAsync` records `importStartedUtc`, counts the selection's blocking/warning findings from `previewFlags` before cloning, and calls `CompleteImport` on all three terminal paths (success with the store's result; cancel; failure with `Safe(e)`). Action handlers: product pool / diagnostics navigate, show rejected/warned set the preview severity filter, retry re-runs the failed progress stage (or the apply), re-read runs `InspectAsync`. **Behaviour change:** the apply no longer auto-navigates to the product pool — the summary stays on the page and the toast/next action offer the pool.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportCompletionSummaryTests.cs` (new, 5, red first): success counts + duration + revision + actions; partial names refused rows, failure and cancel write nothing (rejected = selection); no-op for already-applied feed and all-identical selection; partial selection quotes no hash, actions need their route; error redacted, payload and stack trace never shown, blank result is a failure, duration formatting. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportCompletionWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real window, local three-item feed — Inspect → Preview → select all → Apply ends in Success (3 added, duration > 0, revision with name/rev/hash and no path, panel visible with headline, counts and "Ürün havuzuna git", store has 3 products); Preview → select all → Apply again ends in NoOp from the store's `AlreadyApplied` ("Değişiklik yok", applied 0, still 3 products). **Fixture fact:** `XmlAuthStore` requires a GUID source id (`XmlSourceReader.PathFor`) — a source with a non-GUID id fails the apply with "Kaynak kimliği geçersiz".

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=ImportCompletionSummary.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportCompletionSummaryTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportCompletionWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=501 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 495 + 6 new); artifact SHA-256 c678b157f7b90ae11fc02c565f945f168a9e64b547128619c81fe4078ccffae5 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: Partial cannot arise on the XML path today — `XmlCatalog.Preview` refuses blocking rows before they reach the grid, so a selection never carries a blocking finding and `Rejected` is 0 on success (the model and its test cover Partial for the Excel path or a future collecting preview); the summary is not persisted — `XmlRunRecord` still stores only added/updated/unchanged/error, so the run history cannot show warnings, rejected or duration; the scheduled (`ScheduledAsync`) import raises no summary; the window test does not drive the Failure/Cancelled paths on a live window (model tests cover them; the terminal-path wiring is the same `CompleteImport` call); the toast's action is the first routed action only.

## #829 — XML source list grouping and health scan (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-829-source-list-grouping off codex/issue-828-import-completion-summary (6d086ee)

New issue. DESIGN: the XML source list was one flat ordered list (#823) with no health grouping, no filter, no "when did it last succeed / when will it run next / is it running now", and no way to check every source's reachability at once.

`XmlSourceListGrouping.cs` (new): `SourceHealthBand` in operator order — Running, Problem, Healthy, NeverRun, Disabled, Unsupported. `Classify(source, row, latestRun, now)` from recorded state only: unsupported location → Unsupported; disabled → Disabled (wins over stale health); a `Running` run record with a live lease → Running, with an expired lease → Problem ("askıda"); a non-HEALTHY health state → Problem (state word + redacted error); HEALTHY with latency ≥ 5 000 ms → Problem ("yavaş"); last run Failed/Abandoned → Problem (+ redacted error); `LastFeedState` INCOMPLETE → Problem; never checked and never ran → NeverRun; else Healthy. `Describe` adds last success (relative), `NextSchedule` (from `AutoImport`/`LastRunUtc`/`IntervalMinutes`: "sıradaki kontrolde", "20 dk sonra", "otomatik değil", "pasif") and `ActiveRun` ("sürüyor · 3 dk" / "askıda · …"); `Detail` joins them for the row's second line. `Group(entries, SourceListFilter(band, text))` → bands in order, empty bands dropped, last-used first then title; `Counts` over everything; `Matches` searches the title and the **masked** location only, so a secret in an address cannot be found by searching for it; `LatestRuns` picks the newest record per source from the run store's list. Problem text passes `AuditStore.Redact` + `MarketplaceConnectionStore.RedactSecrets`, a payload/trace is dropped.

Wired: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — `RefreshSources` now builds entries from the #823 rows + `XmlRunStore.List(limit 1000)`, binds the list to a `ListCollectionView` grouped by band label (group headers "Sorunlu (2)" via `SourceGroupHeaderConverter`), keeps the editor's source selected across rebinds under a `changingSourceList` guard (a filter or scan never drops the draft), and renders band chips ("Tümü (N)" + one per non-empty band, bold when selected, `AutomationProperties.Name` with count and selection). Above the list: a search box (title/masked address) and a "Sağlık taraması" button → `ScanSourceHealthAsync` (every enabled supported source through `XmlSourceHealthChecker.CheckAsync`, 4 at a time, 10 s timeout, fields saved as the scheduler does, progress and a final "N kaynak · M sorunlu" line + toast). The row template is now two lines (summary; detail) and the tooltip adds the problem. `sources.IsSynchronizedWithCurrentItem=false` + `MoveCurrentTo(null)`: a view-bound ListBox otherwise selects the view's current (first) item on every rebind, silently, without the page's selection handler. `ImportSourceListWindowTests` (#823) looks containers up by item (grouped lists nest them under group items).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceListGroupingTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): every band from recorded state (running, stale lease, timeout, slow, failed run, incomplete feed, healthy with last success/next schedule, never-run, disabled over old health, unsupported, due now); 100 mixed sources grouped in band order with counts summing to 100, last-used leading its band, band + text filters; secrets in an address or error never reach summary/detail/masked location or the search, a payload error is dropped, `LatestRuns` keeps the newest. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceListWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real window with 106 sources (a live run record, a timeout with a secret query, a slow one, a healthy scheduled one, a local file, a disabled one, 100 healthy fillers) — order by band, headers with counts, second line with "sürüyor" / "son başarı 2 sa önce" / "sonraki: 20 dk sonra" / "zaman aşımı" and no secret, chips with counts, the Sorunlu chip narrowing to 2 while "Tümü (106)" still counts, search for the secret finds nothing but the host finds the row, a HEALTHY check on the local file (offline) moves it into the healthy band, selection kept across refresh, scan wired.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=XmlSourceListGrouping.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceListGroupingTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceListWindowTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ImportSourceListWindowTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=505 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 501 + 4 new); artifact SHA-256 068df79f5b2a7aeeefe9a5f3e99dac223cac8b684daaa3b77c686c751f6c8490 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: "store" grouping is not possible — `XmlSource` has no store/marketplace key, so grouping is by health/enabled/running only; the scan itself is not exercised on a live window (it needs the network for https sources; the window test verifies the checker's offline file result feeds the band and that the method is wired); the band/text filter is not persisted across sessions; the list is not virtualized while grouped (106 rows render fine; thousands would need `VirtualizingPanel.IsVirtualizingWhenGrouping`); the scan does not cancel per source beyond the app lifetime token.

## #830 — XML source detail health panel (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-830-source-health-panel off codex/issue-829-source-list-grouping (d85b9b8)

New issue. DESIGN: the source detail had one hint line ("Kaynak özeti: N ürün · son çalışma … · durum …") — no reachability, latency, drift, validation or retry state, and no single verdict.

`XmlSourceHealthPanel.cs` (new): `Compose(SourceHealthFacts, now)` → `SourceHealthPanelModel(Verdict, Level, Headline, Lines, Actions)`. Verdict ladder from recorded facts only: never checked and never ran → NeverRun ("Hiç çalışmadı"); an unreachable check, a failed/abandoned last run with no success since, or a stopped import stage → Failed ("Başarısız"); slow (≥ 5 000 ms), XML-shape drift (current mapping-shape fingerprint ≠ last applied), mapping drift (`MappingRevision` ≠ `LastAppliedMappingRevision`), incomplete feed, quarantine pending or blocking validation → Degraded ("Kısmen sorunlu"); else Healthy. Seven lines: Erişim (state word + HTTP + when + redacted reason), Gecikme, Son başarı (relative + count), Şema kayması (with "yapı okunmadı" when no preview has run — an unread structure is not drift), Son doğrulama (rows · engel · uyarı · when, from the page's own preview), Yeniden deneme (running / stopped at stage / last run failed or abandoned / gerekmiyor), Havuz (products for this source + quarantine). Actions: Yenile, Erişimi kontrol et, plus Yeniden başlat when Failed and no import runs, Yeniden önizle on drift. `SafeError` masks user information in URLs, `Authorization`/`x-api-key`/`api_key` headers **including the credential after a Basic/Bearer/Digest scheme word**, bare scheme tokens, then `AuditStore.Redact` + `MarketplaceConnectionStore.RedactSecrets`; a payload or stack trace becomes "Ayrıntı tanılama günlüğünde."; the host stays.

Wired: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — `xmlSourceHealthPanel` (headline with glyph + accent border, one DockPanel row per line with label/value/detail and `AutomationProperties.Name`, the old summary line kept inside, action buttons) replaces the bare hint in the source form; `RefreshXmlSourceHealthAsync` gathers count/run/quarantine/persisted on a background task and composes the facts with the page's state (`previewValidation` counts and `previewEvaluatedUtc` when the preview belongs to this source, `previewMappingShapeFingerprint` when the loaded XML is this source's, `importProgress.Failed`, `importRunning`); `CheckSourceReachabilityAsync` runs `XmlSourceHealthChecker.CheckAsync` (10 s), records the result on the draft and the persisted source, refreshes the list and recomposes; Restart → `RetryImportStage()` or a re-read, Preview → `PreviewAsync`. The full read-and-inspect check button remains as "Kaynak sağlığını kontrol et (tam okuma)". `XmlSourceListGrouping.HealthWord` made public for the state words.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceHealthPanelTests.cs` (new, 3, red first — the redaction test failed on the first run: "Authorization: Basic dXNl…" masked the scheme word and left the credential; fixed): verdict ladder healthy/degraded(slow)/failed/never-run with line contents and actions; drift (shape, mapping, unread), validation (warning, blocking), retry (stopped stage, failed run with reason, recovered by a later success, abandoned, running blocks restart), quarantine; error text never carries user info, token, Basic/Bearer credential or x-api-key, payload/trace replaced, host kept. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceHealthPanelWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real window — a timed-out source with a failed run reads Başarısız with "zaman aşımı", latency, "2 gün önce · 8 ürün", "son çalıştırma başarısız" + "Sku", no secret/credential/user info, "Yeniden başlat" offered; a healthy local-file source with a succeeded run reads Sağlıklı with "1 sa önce · 3 ürün", "gerekmiyor", pool "0 ürün", no restart, focusable actions; a fresh source reads Hiç çalışmadı; `CheckSourceReachabilityAsync` on the local file (offline) records a newer `LastHealthCheckUtc` = HEALTHY and the panel shows "erişilebilir · az önce".

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=XmlSourceHealthPanel.cs (new), XmlSourceListGrouping.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceHealthPanelTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/XmlSourceHealthPanelWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=509 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 505 + 4 new); artifact SHA-256 80012ba60508f3365408dc899a343e442864be2fe10e5bafdec28219d9fb85ab (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: schema drift can only be judged after a preview in this session (the source records the last applied shape but not the current one — a scheduled drift check would need to store the fingerprint of every read); the run store's status literal is "Succeeded" and only Failed/Abandoned count as failures — a run with an unknown status is treated as neither; the panel is recomposed on selection, after a check and after an import, not on a timer; the window test drives Failed/Healthy/NeverRun and the check action but not Degraded on a live window (model tests cover it); the Restart action is not exercised on a live window.

## #831 — XML preview sticky action toolbar (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-831-preview-sticky-toolbar off codex/issue-830-source-health-panel (273be7e)

New issue. DESIGN: the preview's apply/recompute buttons lived in the page-top action row, far from the grid, with no affected counts, no selection validation state and no freshness word next to them; nothing told the operator why apply would be refused.

`PreviewActionToolbar.cs` (new): `Compose(PreviewToolbarState)` → `PreviewToolbarModel`. Counts line "S / R seçili · etkilenen N (yeni, değişen) · aynı" (+ "filtre: K görünür" when a filter hides rows — the total is never hidden); validation "engel · uyarı (seçili)" with a severity level, "seçim yok" when nothing is selected; status "Önizleme güncel" / "Önizleme geçersiz: <stepper reason>" / "Önizleme hesaplanmadı" / "XML okunmadı" / "Aktarım sürüyor · iptal edilebilir". `CanApply` only for a fresh preview with a selection carrying no blocking finding while nothing runs; otherwise `ApplyReason` spells out which rule refused (read XML / compute preview / stale / select rows / N blocking rows / running). `ApplyLabel` names the count and says "yerel". Cancel only while running; recompute whenever XML is loaded and nothing runs.

Wired: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — `previewToolbar` (apply, cancel, recompute buttons + counts, validation, status texts with `SeverityStyle` glyphs/accent/border) docked as the top band of the preview GroupBox above the filter bar and the grid (`Dock(previewToolbar, previewTop, preview)`), so it stays put while the grid scrolls. `RefreshPreviewToolbar()` composes from the cached flow state (`lastFlowState`, set by `RefreshImportStepper` — a selection change never re-scans the XML), the stepper's stale reason and the selection's `previewFlags`; called from the stepper refresh, the filter refresh and `preview.SelectionChanged`. The apply button runs the same `ImportAsync` (its revision check, idempotency and local-only write untouched); disabled state shows the reason as tooltip (`ToolTipService.ShowOnDisabled`) and `AutomationProperties.HelpText`; the cancel button cancels the running stage's `importCts` as the progress panel's does.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PreviewActionToolbarTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): fresh clean selection applies with counts/validation/status and the local label, filter note; every refused state carries its reason (no selection, blocking rows, stale with the stepper text, no preview, no XML, running with cancel). `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PreviewActionToolbarWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real window, 300-row local feed (every tenth row without description) — before read "XML okunmadı", after read "hesaplanmadı", after preview + select all "Önizleme güncel", "300 / 300 seçili · etkilenen 300 (300 yeni, 0 değişen) · 0 aynı", "0 engel · 30 uyarı (seçili)", apply enabled with "(300)"; scrolling the grid to row 300 leaves the toolbar's position unchanged and visible; buttons focusable; clearing the selection disables apply with "satır seçin"; bumping the draft's markup makes the preview stale — toolbar warns, apply disabled, and `ImportAsync` itself still throws "önizleme geçersiz" with nothing written; recompute → fresh and enabled again.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=PreviewActionToolbar.cs (new), MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PreviewActionToolbarTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PreviewActionToolbarWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=512 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 509 + 3 new); artifact SHA-256 fa4c4e77d5cef871d23f2317d2755695453828a1bfae488d53d1b6dc8023b631 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the page-top "Önizleme hesapla" / "Seçilileri havuza al" buttons are still there (two ways to apply; both run the same guarded `ImportAsync`) — removing them touches the stepper's jump targets and was left for a later pass; the blocking-selection refusal cannot occur on the XML path today (`XmlCatalog.Preview` refuses blocking rows first) so it is covered by the model test only; the cancel button in the toolbar is not driven on a live window (the progress panel's identical cancel is covered by #827's model tests); the toolbar does not persist a preferred filter.

## #832 — XML diff colour-independent semantics (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-832-diff-colour-independent off codex/issue-831-preview-sticky-toolbar (b6a1252)

New issue. DESIGN: the only before/after renderer (the #805 content preview) told "Önce" from "Sonra" by two hard-coded RGB colours and had no notion of added / removed / unchanged; the XML preview had no field-level diff at all — a row said "değişen" but not which fields, or what they would become.

`FieldDiff.cs` (new): `DiffKind {Added, Removed, Changed, Unchanged}` from the raw values (`Classify`: empty→present added, present→empty removed, both empty unchanged), `Row`/`Build<T>` (a null "before" means nothing existed: every present field is added), `ProductFields` (title, description, brand, category, barcode, GTIN, cost, price, stock, image count), `PresentValue` (the one owner of value presentation: `AuditStore.Sanitize` masks tokens/credentials, flattened to one line, capped at 400 with a truncation flag, "(boş)" for empty), `Present(kind, highContrast)` → glyph ＋ － △ ＝, word eklendi / kaldırıldı / değişti / aynı, border weight 2 / 2 / 1.5 / 0.5 and an accent from `SeverityStyle` (Success / Blocking / Warning / Info — `SystemColors` under high contrast), `Summary`. `FieldDiffRenderer.cs` (new): one focusable, tab-stop `Border` per row (glyph + field + word header, "Önce"/"Sonra" lines that wrap, "(kısaltıldı)" when capped, an added field shows no before and a removed one no after, an unchanged row shows its value quietly), `AutomationProperties.Name` reading the whole row, a summary line on top. `ProductContentDiff` now carries `Kind` on each row and delegates its presentation to `FieldDiff.PresentValue`; `OpenContentPreview` (MainWindow.Filters.cs) renders through `FieldDiffRenderer` — the RGB-only colours are gone.

Wired for XML: `MainWindow.xaml.cs` — "Satır farkı" in the preview toolbar (enabled when a preview exists and exactly one row is selected), `BuildPreviewRowDiff()` compares the selected preview row with the pool product it would touch (`ExistingProductFor`: SKU, else barcode — the import's own match order) through `FieldDiff.Build(existing, row, ProductFields, includeUnchanged: true)` in a `DialogShell` titled "Satır farkı · <sku>" (redacted), with a note whether a match exists; nothing is written.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FieldDiffTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): kinds from raw values, an existing-vs-incoming product build with the expected kind per field and a summary whose counts add up, a new product all added; four distinct glyphs and words, border weights ordered, every high-contrast accent a system colour; a value with a token masked, flattened, "(boş)", a 1 000-char value capped with the flag, and the content preview's rows carrying kinds. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FieldDiffWindowTests.cs` (new, 1): real window, pool seeded through `store.Import` (S001 with an old title, a description carrying a pasted token plus 600 characters, price 10, stock 5) and a two-row feed — the diff button is off without a preview and with two rows selected; for S001 the dialog has one focusable tab-stop border per product field: "△ Başlık · değişti" with Önce/Sonra, "－ Açıklama · kaldırıldı" capped with "(kısaltıldı)" and no token, "＋ Marka · eklendi" without a before, price and stock changed, "＝ Barkod · aynı", the removal's border heavier than the unchanged row's, the automation name reading "Başlık: değişti", the pool untouched; for S002 every row is added or unchanged and the note says no match. **Fixture facts:** `CatalogStore.SaveProduct` only updates ("Ürün bulunamadı") — seed pools through `store.Import(source, rows)`; a dialog that is built but not shown has no visual tree — walk `LogicalTreeHelper.GetChildren`.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=FieldDiff.cs (new), FieldDiffRenderer.cs (new), ProductContentDiff.cs, MainWindow.Filters.cs, MainWindow.xaml.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FieldDiffTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/FieldDiffWindowTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=516 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 512 + 4 new); artifact SHA-256 048d92fa73aef050e97eed0d4e226842b10de304fdf3981f5251543eccd2abbb (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the diff is per selected row on demand, not a column in the preview grid (a "which fields change" cell would need the pool lookup per row at preview time — the #825 `Change` flag stays row-level); "colour-blind simulation" was not run as an image test — the guarantee is structural (glyph + word + border weight distinct per kind, verified by tests), not a rendered-pixel check; the content preview's rows for fields outside `ProductContentDiff.Fields` (price, stock) are not shown there — that dialog is still about customer-facing text; the row diff does not apply the import's field-lock rules (`LockPrice` etc.), so a locked field shows as "değişti" even though the import would keep it.

## #833 — Generic mapping required-field indicators (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-833-excel-mapping-required-indicators off codex/issue-832-diff-colour-independent (b7db1b1)

New issue. DESIGN: the generic (Excel) column-mapping dialog was thirteen bare label + combo rows — no required marker, no type, no sample, no missing count, no jump, and "Eşlemeyi kullan" accepted a mapping that could not import. (The XML mapping grid got all of this in #824 and is out of scope here, as the issue says.)

`ExcelMappingDialog.cs` (new): `Build(owner, headers, sampleFor, existing)` → `ExcelMappingDialogView(Window, Combos, Summary, FirstProblem, Confirm, Table, Result)`. Every target field row carries the #824 semantics through `ImportMappingTable.Compose` (same `RequiredGroups`: SKU **or** barcode, and name): a required marker naming its alternative ("✱ zorunlu (veya barkod)") or "isteğe bağlı", the expected type, a masked sample from the first data row (`ImportMappingTable.SafeSample`: PII → "[pii-email]", tokens redacted, payloads hidden, 60 chars), and a status line with the reason ("✖ zorunlu eksik", "⚠ çakışma" when one header serves two fields, "○ boş (isteğe bağlı)"), accent and border weight from `SeverityStyle`. The summary counts ("3 zorunlu eksik", "N isteğe bağlı boş — eşleme hazır."), "İlk soruna git" focuses the first problem's combo, and the confirm is enabled only when nothing blocks (tooltip says why otherwise). Labels wrap at 220 DIP and combos are capped at 230 DIP with the full header as tooltip, so a 130-character header neither widens the dialog nor hides the combo; combos are focusable tab stops with `AutomationProperties.Name` carrying required/status/reason. `CatalogExcel.FirstRow(path)` (new) reads header → first data row cell text as the specimen source.

Wired: `MainWindow.Excel.cs` — `BuildExcelMappingDialog(path, existing)` reads headers and the first row and builds the view (a first-row read failure is logged and leaves samples empty); "Kolonları elle eşle" → `ShowMappingDialog(path, headers, existing)` shows it modally and returns `Result` only on confirm; the old static builder with hard-coded rows is gone.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExcelMappingDialogTests.cs` (new, 3, red first — the first run caught that the e-mail sample is redacted to "[pii-email]" entirely, which is right; the assertions were fixed): missing required — 3 counted (SKU + barcode group, name) and named, confirm off, jump visible and focusing the SKU combo on a shown window, the mail sample masked (no token, no address), required text with its alternative and an optional marker; mapping SKU + name makes it ready and confirm on; the same header on two fields is a conflict on both and blocks again. Optional-only gaps: ready, "isteğe bağlı boş", no jump, combos focusable tab stops, labels wrap, the 130-char header keeps the combo ≤ 230 DIP with the full header as tooltip, dialog width ≤ 620 DIP. Real path: a ClosedXML workbook (product_code / title / price / mail + one data row) — `CatalogExcel.FirstRow` reads it; the window's `BuildExcelMappingDialog` yields samples from the workbook ("ABC-1"; mail masked), "1 zorunlu eksik" with `FirstProblemKey` = Name, confirm off until the name is mapped, and the confirmed `Result` carries 1-based columns for Sku/Name/Description.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=ExcelMappingDialog.cs (new), Catalog/CatalogExcel.cs, MainWindow.Excel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ExcelMappingDialogTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=519 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 516 + 3 new); artifact SHA-256 a32a57a83b0eb8621ea8880c3b177f6d60765ac3675567f8403c9352dbdaccec (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: the "validation deep-link" goes to the mapping row, not into the preview's per-row errors (the Excel preview errors are sentences in `ExcelPreview.Errors`; linking a rejected row back to the field it failed on would need the #826 parser's field applied to the grid); the automatic header-alias mapping (`ExcelColumnMapping.FromProfile`) still runs outside this dialog and is not shown with the same markers on the page until the dialog is opened; the modal path (`ShowDialog` → `Result`) is exercised through the confirm click on a built dialog, not through a modal loop; samples come from the first data row only.

## #834 — Order list column presets (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-834-order-column-presets off codex/issue-833-excel-mapping-required-indicators (b3110db)

New issue. DESIGN: the order grid had thirteen columns and no way to switch between an operations, a shipping and a finance view without hiding columns one by one; nothing was remembered across a restart.

`OrderColumnPresets.cs` (new): three presets built only from the grid's real binding paths — Operasyon (marketplace, shop, order, source status, payment, stock decision, delivery, SLA), Kargo (order, shop, delivery, SLA, carriers, tracking, last sync), Finans (salt okunur) (marketplace, shop, order, payment, total, source, last sync) — plus "Özel". `Resolve(preset, liveKeys, currentWidths)` → a `DataGridLayoutState` (#792 codec): preset columns visible in preset order, every other live column hidden after them, widths kept (a preset never resizes), a column the grid lost skipped, a column it gained hidden, and any PII key (`PiiKeys`: customer/buyer name, e-mail, phone, addresses — none exist in the grid today) collapsed under every preset. `OrderColumnPresetController(get, set)`: remembers the preset key (`layout:orders:preset`) and, separately, the user's custom layout (`layout:orders:custom`); `Select(preset)` applies without touching the custom layout, `Select("custom")` restores it, `UserEdited(layout)` flips to custom and stores the layout; an unknown remembered key falls back to custom.

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — a "Kolonlar" combo (`AutomationProperties.Name` "Kolon ön ayarı") in the toolbar; `ApplyLayout` (display index, width, visibility through `DataGridLayoutCodec.ResolveOrder`, PII collapsed under a preset), `CurrentColumns`, `UserEditedLayout` hooked to `ColumnDisplayIndexChanged`/`ColumnReordered` and the Width/Visibility dependency-property descriptors of every column (guarded while a layout is being applied), `Initial` applied once the columns exist, the combo synced to the controller's mode; the store is the panel's existing `UiPreferenceStore`.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): presets name only live columns and never PII, options order, the shipping preset resolves to its columns in order with hidden ones after and widths kept, an unknown preset is null; schema change — a lost column skipped, a gained column hidden, a PII column collapsed under every preset. Controller: nothing remembered → custom with nothing to apply; a user edit becomes the custom layout; choosing a preset leaves it byte-for-byte; back to custom restores it with its sort; an edit under a preset flips to custom with the new width; unknown key throws; a restarted controller remembers the preset and keeps the custom layout; garbage falls back to custom. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real `OrdersPanel` — Özel with everything visible, Kargo applies its columns in order without resizing OrderId and writes no custom layout, hiding SLA flips to Özel and stores the custom layout, a rebuilt panel (restart) comes back as Özel with the shipping columns minus SLA, Operasyon applies and leaves the custom bytes untouched, a third build remembers Operasyon and Özel restores the custom layout; the grid carries no PII column.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=OrderColumnPresets.cs (new), OrdersPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsUiTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=522 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 519 + 3 new); artifact SHA-256 412dd222dc7275bb4c19ae0ea9096f9270747b5e4a7f122188437c91a8c08df2 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: a column change made while a preset is active becomes the custom layout (the user's own action) — the previous custom layout is replaced by that newer one, not kept as a second copy; the order grid's sort is not part of a preset (only the custom layout keeps a sort, and the panel does not yet capture the grid's live sort into it); the presets are fixed — no user-defined presets beyond "Özel"; the DPI claim rests on the codec's DIP widths (#792), not on a rendered test at 200 %.

## #835 — Order list urgency sorting (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-835-order-urgency-sorting off codex/issue-834-order-column-presets (2bb4c74)

New issue. DESIGN: the order list had per-column states (SLA, stock decision, sync, delivery) but no single urgency to sort or filter by; the operator had to read every row.

`OrderUrgency.cs` (new): `OrderUrgencyScorer.Score(OrderUrgencyInput)` → `OrderUrgency(Score, Band, Reasons)` from recorded states only — a delivery exception (+40) or return (+25), a delayed package (+30 plus a day over the #789 SLA, capped), pending exceptions from the exception store (+20, +5 each extra, capped), items no catalogue product matches (+20, +5 each extra, capped), no sync (+10) or a sync older than 24 h (+10), and a paid order unshipped for a day or more (+10 +2/day, capped). A cancelled order ("cancel"/"iptal", Turkish-aware lower-casing) is band "iptal" at 0 and nothing else counts. Bands acil ≥ 60, yüksek ≥ 30, normal ≥ 10, düşük. A missing source timestamp earns nothing and is named ("zaman damgası yok"). `InputFor(order, knownSkus, pendingExceptions, now)` uses the same `OrdersRules.EvaluateSla` the SLA column uses; `Sort(items, score, key)` is highest score first, then the key ordinal — the same order on every run. Reasons are generic words with counts: no title, name, SKU or order id ever reaches the label. `OrderSnapshot` gained `[JsonIgnore] UrgencyScore/UrgencyLabel` (computed, never serialized).

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — `Load()` scores every order from the catalogue's SKUs and the exception store's pending records (a store failure leaves the counts at 0, never blocks the list); `Filter()` adds an "Aciliyet bandı" combo (Tümü / Acil / Yüksek / Normal / Düşük / İptal) and an "Aciliyete göre sırala" toggle (on by default) applied after every other filter, including the store filter; an "Aciliyet" column shows the label; the Operasyon preset (#834) now includes it (the #834 tests' expectations updated accordingly).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderUrgencyTests.cs` (new, 3, red first — the first run caught that an invariant lower of "İptal" never matches "iptal"; fixed with Turkish culture lower-casing): states add up into bands (quiet 0, delayed + no sync = yüksek, exception + pending + unmapped + stale = acil, four days unshipped = normal, in transit within SLA = 0), a cancelled order is 0/"iptal" whatever else it carries (incl. "İptal edildi"), band thresholds, same input → same score/band/reasons; ties sort by key regardless of arrival order; a missing timestamp earns nothing and is named; `InputFor` counts blank and unknown SKUs as unmapped, evaluates the SLA, and the label carries no title/name/SKU/id. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderUrgencyUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real `OrdersPanel` with five manual orders in two shops (delayed, exception + unmapped item titled with a customer name, delivered, cancelled with an exception, and a tie of the delayed one) and a seeded catalogue — the list opens as [b-exc, a-late, e-tie, c-ok, d-cancel] (score, then key), labels read "acil · teslimat sorunu · 1 eşlenmemiş kalem" without the name or SKU, "gecikti", "iptal" at 0, a manual delivered order is "normal · senkron yok"; the Aciliyet column exists; the shop filter keeps the order; band filters Acil/İptal/Yüksek narrow correctly; the sort toggle is focusable and off yields the same set.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=OrderUrgency.cs (new), OrdersModels.cs, OrdersPanel.cs, OrderColumnPresets.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderUrgencyTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderUrgencyUiTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsTests.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderColumnPresetsUiTests.cs, IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=526 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 522 + 4 new); artifact SHA-256 50143731d08dfaa78c9f07348aef08ab2f33a149aeebe44724fa1e861aa0ffa9 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: "failed sync" is modelled as no sync / stale sync — the order store keeps no per-order sync failure record to score from; the weights are fixed (no per-store tuning); the score is recomputed only on `Load()` (refresh/reload), not on a timer, so "days waiting" ages until the next load; the band filter and sort toggle are not persisted in the saved views; the SLA's max transit days is the #789 default for every carrier.

### #835 fix-up (2026-09-13): band labels were derived with the running culture

CI (en-US runner) failed `OrderUrgencyUiTests` on the "İptal" band filter: the combo's labels were built as `char.ToUpper(band[0], CurrentCulture) + rest`, which under en-US turns "iptal" into "Iptal", so the page offered a label the test (and a Turkish user) would not find, and selecting "İptal" left the previous filter in place. Fix: `OrderUrgencyScorer.BandOptions` — a fixed key/label table ("Acil", "Yüksek", "Normal", "Düşük", "İptal") — feeds the combo, and the filter resolves the band by key from the selected index, never by label. New test `BandLabelsAreFixedTurkishCapitalsWhateverTheRunningCulture` runs under en-US and pins both the labels and the reason. TEST_RESULT=527 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; 526 + 1); artifact SHA-256 8fe278a4940115b44baebf039b1a56d964022f1083bd926fd55be62dabbded09. The queued CI run itself sat unscheduled for ~50 minutes and was cancelled + re-run to get a runner (recorded as a process note).

## #836 — Order workspace split-pane layout (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-836-order-split-pane off codex/issue-835-order-urgency-sorting (dd32a19)

New issue. DESIGN: the order workspace was a fixed two-column grid (list star, detail 370 px) — no resizing, no memory of a width, no fallback on a narrow window, and every list rebind (search, filter, reload) dropped the selection and the detail with it.

`OrderWorkspaceLayout.cs` (new): `Mode(width)` — side by side from 900 DIP, stacked (detail under the list) below; `ClampDetailWidth(requested, workspace)` — never under 280 DIP, never so wide the list drops under 480, never more than 45 % of the workspace (1024 → 460.8 max; 4K → 1500 allowed; 700 → 280), nonsense → default 370; `Serialize`/`Deserialize` — a plain invariant DIP number, garbage or absurd values read as the default; `SelectionKey(order)` (marketplace + shop + id) and `Restore(listed, key)` — the same order among fresh instances; `CanOpenDetail(order, marketplaceFilter, shopFilter)` — with a filter set, an order from another store never opens.

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — the workspace grid is tagged "order-split" with list | `GridSplitter` (focusable, arrow-key increment 16, `AutomationProperties.Name`) | detail; `ApplyWorkspaceMode(width)` rebuilds column vs row definitions on `SizeChanged` (stacked: rows star / auto / 45 %*), applied once at build; a drag end clamps and persists the detail width to the panel's `UiPreferenceStore` (`layout:orders:split`), restored on the next build. `Filter()` remembers the selected order's key before rebinding and re-selects the same order among the new instances; when it is no longer listed the detail is closed with a reason (a detail never shows an order the list does not). The selection handler refuses to open a detail outside the current marketplace/shop filter (status message, selection cleared). The Etsy refresh no longer blanks the detail unconditionally — the reload restores the selection or closes it with the reason.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderWorkspaceLayoutTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): mode at 1024/4K/899/unmeasured; clamping at 1024 (share), minimum, 4K (1500 allowed, share capped), 700 (detail to its minimum so the list keeps 480), NaN inputs; serialize/deserialize incl. "", "abc", 10, 99999, NaN; selection key/restore across fresh instances (same id in another shop is another order); `CanOpenDetail` with Tümü/null, case-insensitive marketplace, exact shop, refusals. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderWorkspaceLayoutUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel in a window — 3 columns at 1400 with the default width and a focusable keyboard splitter, the list at ≥ 480; at 800 three rows with the detail in row 2 and the splitter resizing rows; back at 1400 three columns; a drag-completed persists "450"; a search rebind keeps o-2 selected with its detail; a reveal reload selects o-1 with fresh instances and a store the list lacks is not revealed; a shop filter on S2 clears the S1 selection and closes its detail with "ayrıntı kapatıldı", then an S2 order opens; a rebuilt panel restores the 450 DIP width.

Not done: the stacked (narrow) mode's row split is not persisted (only the side-by-side detail width is); the DPI claim rests on DIP arithmetic and a 1400/800 DIP window, not on a rendered test at 200 %; "wrong store" is judged against the list's own marketplace/shop filters — the shell's connected-store list (`AllowedStoreKeys`, #813) is not consulted by the panel itself, only by the reveal path in the window; a drag is simulated by raising `DragCompleted` after setting the column width, not by mouse input; the selection is remembered for the panel's lifetime, not across a restart.

## #837 — Order detail header hierarchy (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-837-order-detail-header off codex/issue-836-order-split-pane (2d11cb8)

New issue. DESIGN: the order detail opened with a plain "Sipariş ayrıntısı" title inside the scrolling body — no store, no status, no payment, no SLA, no exception count at a glance, and all of it scrolled away with the body.

`OrderDetailHeader.cs` (new): `Compose(order, pendingExceptions, now)` → three tiers. Title: the order id, elided in the middle past 24 characters (both ends kept) with the full id for the tooltip and the automation name; "(numarasız)" when blank. Secondary: channel · store · status word (Ödendi / Gönderildi / İptal / capped raw status / "Durum yok"; "kanal yok" / "mağaza yok" when blank). Tertiary: payment ("Ödeme bilgisi yok" for blank or the default "Bilinmiyor"), delivery SLA (the #789 `SlaLabelAt`, "Paket yok" without shipments, "SLA değerlendirilmez" on a cancelled order), exception summary ("N bekleyen istisna" / "istisna yok"). Level and glyph follow the worst fact: delivery exception or delay → Blocking ✖, pending exceptions or a return → Warning ⚠, cancelled → Info ℹ, else Success ✔. Nothing reads an item, a buyer or an address.

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — the detail column is now a `DockPanel` host ("order-detail-host"): the header `Border` ("order-detail-header") docked top, the scrolling body filling the rest, so the header stays put however far the body scrolls; `RenderDetailHeader` draws title (18 pt, `CharacterEllipsis`, tooltip "Sipariş <full id>", accent), secondary (13 pt, wrap), tertiary (11 pt, wrap), an accent bottom border weighted by `SeverityStyle`, and `AutomationProperties.Name` = the full text; `Edit()` composes it from the pending-exception counts `Load()` now keeps (`pendingByOrder`), a new local order gets no header, and the detail-closed path (#836) collapses it. The #836 window test now finds the body through the tagged host.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderDetailHeaderTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): tiers from the order (id, "etsy · S1 · Ödendi", "Ödeme: Ödendi · Yolda (2 gün) · istisna yok", Success ✔), no item/buyer text; delayed → Blocking with "Gecikti" and "2 bekleyen istisna"; pending exceptions alone → Warning; an Exception package → Blocking "Teslimat sorunu"; a 41-character id elided to 24 with both ends and the full id in the automation text; missing payment named, "Paket yok", "Gönderildi"; a cancelled order ("İptal edildi", 30 days in transit) → "İptal", "SLA değerlendirilmez", Info ℹ; a blank order → "(numarasız)", "kanal yok · mağaza yok · Durum yok"; exactly 24 characters not elided. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderDetailHeaderUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel in a window — no header until an order opens; the header is the host's first child, above the body; opening o-1 shows id, "etsy · S1 · Ödendi", "Ödeme bilgisi yok", "Yolda", "istisna yok" and no item/buyer text; scrolling the body to its end leaves the header's position unchanged and visible; a 44-character cancelled order shows an elided title with the full id as tooltip and automation name, "İptal", "SLA değerlendirilmez", `CharacterEllipsis`; at 800 DIP the header stays inside its column and the lower tiers wrap; filtering to another store closes the detail and hides the header.

## #838 — Order line reconciliation visuals (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-838-order-line-reconciliation off codex/issue-837-order-detail-header (b86e09f)

New issue. DESIGN: the detail listed lines as "N × title · SKU" text — no shipped, returned or cancelled quantity per line, no difference, no over/under state; the operator had to open the stock receipt and the return ledger separately.

`OrderLineReconciliation.cs` (new): `Build(order, receipt, returnedBySku)` → one `OrderLineView` per SKU (lines of one SKU merge — the receipt and the ledger know SKUs, not lines) with ordered (the lines), shipped (what the stock receipt deducted — the only per-SKU fulfilment the app records), returned (the #788 ledger), cancelled (the whole order when its status says so) and the state their difference means: Pending "○ sevk bekliyor", PartiallyShipped "▲ eksik sevk · N adet bekliyor", Shipped "＝ tam sevk" (with "N adet fazla düşüldü" when more left stock than was ordered), PartiallyReturned "◐ kısmi iade · r / s geri geldi" (+ "N adet hiç sevk edilmedi" when both happen on one line), Returned "↩ iade edildi", OverReturned "▼ fazla iade · N adet sevk edilenden fazla geri geldi" (Blocking — the conflict that must never be silent), Cancelled "⊘ iptal" (noting shipped units still open). `Outstanding` is never negative. Titles are redacted and capped at 60; nothing reads a buyer. `Summary` counts lines, over-return conflicts, under-shipments, returns and a cancelled order. `CatalogStore.OrderReturnsApplied(marketplace, shop, orderId)` (new, in `Catalog/OrderReturnReconciliation.cs`) reads the ledger's per-SKU sums — the same rows the reconciliation counts.

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — `Edit()` replaces the text lines of a saved order with a read-only lines `DataGrid` ("order-lines": SKU · Ürün · Sipariş · Sevk · İade · İptal · Açık · Durum, DIP widths) whose rows carry a left accent border weighted by `SeverityStyle`, the state label as tooltip and an `AutomationProperties.Name` reading all four quantities and the state, under a summary line coloured by the worst state; a new local order keeps the plain lines (nothing is recorded yet).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderLineReconciliationTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught a negative `Outstanding` on a cancelled shipped line; clamped): partial shipment, partial return, full return and over-return are four distinct states with four markers on one order, summary counts, pending without a receipt, full shipment, over-deduction named; a cancelled order cancels every line with shipped units noted and nothing outstanding, two lines of one SKU merge, a blank SKU reads "(SKU yok)", a 200-character title with a token is capped and redacted. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderLineReconciliationUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel, real records — the catalogue seeded through the store's import, a stock receipt through `ApplyOrderStock` (A×2 of 3, B×2), a return through `ApplyOrderReturn` (A×1, approved) — the grid shows A as "kısmi iade" with "1 adet hiç sevk edilmedi", B "＝ tam sevk", C "○ sevk bekliyor" with 1 outstanding, a capped title, a focusable 8-column grid with absolute widths, the summary "3 satır · 1 eksik sevk · 1 iadeli"; a cancelled order shows every line cancelled.

Not done: "shipped" is the stock receipt's deduction, not a carrier-confirmed per-line shipment — the app records no per-line shipment quantities, so a package split across lines cannot be shown per package; the over-return conflict cannot be produced through the store (`ApplyOrderReturn` blocks it) and is covered by the model test only; line titles are the source's product text (a personalised item title may carry a name the marketplace put there — it is capped and redacted for secrets, not name-scrubbed); the grid is rebuilt on every `Edit()`, not refreshed when the ledger changes underneath an open detail.

## #839 — Order shipping section hierarchy (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-839-order-shipping-section off codex/issue-838-order-line-reconciliation (9d2e7e7)

New issue. DESIGN: the detail's shipping part was a package picker whose entries read "id · carrier · tracking · state" and an editor for the picked one — no overview of how many packages, which is late or troubled, whether a tracking number is duplicated, and the full tracking number sat in every label.

`OrderShippingSection.cs` (new): `Compose(order, trackingSeenElsewhere, now)` → headline ("Paket yok" / "1 paket" / "N paket · bölünmüş gönderi", plus "N gecikmiş", "N sorunlu", "N teslim", "N işaretli"), one `ShipmentSummaryRow` per package (carrier or "taşıyıcı yok", masked tracking, observed state, the #789 SLA label/status, a marker + word — ✖ gecikti, ✖ teslimat sorunu, ↩ iade, ✔ teslim edildi, → yolda, ○ gönderilmedi — and flags: "takip no yok", "aynı takip no bu siparişte iki kez", "takip no N başka siparişte de var" from the duplicate-tracking anomaly; a flag lifts a quiet package to Warning), notes for the two duplicate cases, the level of the worst package. `MaskTracking` keeps the first two and last four characters (nine or more; shorter shown whole), `MaskTrackingUrl` keeps scheme, host and path only. Tracking keys are matched case-insensitively whatever comparer the caller's dictionary uses (the first test run caught that).

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — `Load()` runs `OrderAnomalyDetector.FindDuplicateTracking(all)` once into `trackingSeenElsewhere`; `Edit()` renders the section ("order-shipping-section": accent left border, headline with glyph, one focusable `Button` row per package whose activation selects that package in the picker, `AutomationProperties.Name` reading state/carrier/masked tracking/SLA/flags, notes) above the existing picker and editor, which still hold the full values.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderShippingSectionTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): no shipment → "Paket yok" with the note; a three-package order → "3 paket · bölünmüş gönderi · 1 gecikmiş · 1 teslim · 1 işaretli", Blocking from the delayed one, ✖/✔/○ markers, "takip no yok" and "taşıyıcı yok" lifted to Warning, the masked number in the line and the full one absent; the same number twice (differing case) flags both packages and a number another order carries is flagged without counting the order itself, two notes; one Exception package → "1 paket · 1 sorunlu"; masking rules incl. a link's query string dropped; an item title with a name and a link with a name never reach the section. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderShippingSectionUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel with a split shipment (delayed + delivered), a second order sharing a tracking number and an order without packages — the section headline, two focusable rows with the expected lines (masked numbers), the shared number flagged from the real detector, the mixing note, the first package selected by default and a row's activation selecting the second (its full tracking number in the editor field), "Paket yok" with no rows for the empty order.

## #840 — Order returns section hierarchy (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-840-order-returns-section off codex/issue-839-order-shipping-section (49980d8)

New issue. DESIGN: an order's returns lived in three places — the exception queue (requests), the return ledger (applied events) and the reconciliation dialog (refund/stock arithmetic) — and the order detail showed none of them.

`OrderReturnsSection.cs` (new): `Compose(order, requests, events, receipt, previewReturn)` → headline ("İade yok" / "N talep (M açık) · K kayıtlı iade · fazla/tam/kısmi counts"), request rows from the exception queue's Return/Cancel records with a marker and word per status (⏳ bekliyor, ◔ önizleme hazır, ✔ çözüldü, ⊘ reddedildi) and the date — never the request's free-text message (it may name a customer; it stays in the queue), each SKU's return position (ordered, fulfilled from the receipt — or the ordered quantity when nothing was deducted, mirroring the reconciliation's cap — returned from the ledger, what can still come back, and "iade edilirse stoğa dönecek N" from the real `PreviewOrderReturn` with a synthetic zero-refund event; states ○ iade yok / ◐ kısmi iade / ↩ tam iade / ▼ fazla iade), the applied events oldest first, and the refund line — refunded against the order total (Success below, Warning at exactly the total, Blocking with "sipariş toplamını aşıyor" above; Info without a total) plus a note when an event's currency differs from the order's. `SafeMessage` redacts, flattens and caps request text and hides payloads. `CatalogStore.OrderReturnEvents` (new) reads the ledger rows of one order oldest first (`OrderReturnLedgerRow`).

Wired: `OrdersPanel.cs` — `Edit()` renders the section ("order-returns-section", a focusable tab stop with an accent left border) under the lines grid for a saved order: headline with glyph, focusable request rows ("order-return-request"), one line per SKU ("order-return-line"), the timeline ("order-return-event"), the refund line ("order-return-refund"), notes, and an automation name of headline + refund; the exception store, the ledger and the reconciliation preview are each read defensively (a failure leaves that part empty, never blocks the detail).

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderReturnsSectionTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first compile caught a shadowed `level`): no return (headline, "İade tutarı yok", the preview's stock figure for a full return), partial with a pending and a rejected request (headline counts, ◐ line with 2 returnable, refund 5/20 Success, request words and levels, a name in the request message absent from the line, one timeline event, Warning overall), full (↩, nothing returnable so no preview asked, refund at the total = Warning, ✔ resolved, oldest-first), no receipt caps at the ordered quantity; over-refund (⚠ line, Blocking, ▼ over-return, note), a foreign-currency event (note, Warning), no order total (Info), `SafeMessage` blank/payload/long/multiline with a token. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderReturnsSectionUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel with a real receipt (A×3), a real applied return (A×1, 5 TRY of 20) and two real exception records (Rejected with a name and a token, Pending) — headline "2 talep (1 açık) · 1 kayıtlı iade · 1 kısmi", two focusable request rows with their words and neither the token nor the name, the SKU line "◐ A · kısmi iade (1 / 3) · kalan iade edilebilir 2 · iade edilirse stoğa dönecek 2" from the real preview, one timeline event, "İade tutarı 5,00 / 20,00 TRY", the section a tab stop, and the stock untouched by rendering (28); an order without returns reads "İade yok" / "İade tutarı yok".

Not done: the section is read-only — applying or rejecting a return still happens in the exceptions panel (this section names the state; it offers no action, so no live-write guard was needed or added); "rejected" is the exception queue's status word, there is no per-request timeline beyond the created date; the over-return and over-refund states cannot be produced through the store (`ApplyOrderReturn` blocks both) and are covered by the model tests only; the stock-impact preview asks the reconciliation once per SKU with a zero refund, so a refund-driven block (currency) would not show there; the section is rebuilt on `Edit()`, not when the ledger changes underneath an open detail.

## #841 — Customer PII reveal affordance (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-841-customer-pii-reveal off codex/issue-840-order-returns-section (c3297b5)

New issue. DESIGN: the order model held no customer contact data at all (the Etsy mapping takes only status and paid; `OrderSnapshot` has no name, e-mail, phone or address), so there was neither a customer section nor a reveal rule. The issue asks for both: fields masked by default, an explicit reveal when a policy allows it, a reason, auto re-mask, and an audit.

`PiiReveal.cs` (new): `OrderCustomer(Marketplace, ShopId, OrderId, Name, Email, Phone, Address)` — a record kept **apart from the order snapshot**, so nothing that serialises orders (`ReadAll`, the transfer export, logs) ever carries it; `PiiRevealPolicy(Allowed=false, RevealSeconds=30, RequireReason=true)` with seconds clamped to 5–300; masks — name to initials ("A••• Y•••"), e-mail to first letter + domain initial + suffix ("a••@e•••.com"), phone to its last two digits ("+•••••••••67"), address to its first word ("Bağdat •••"); `Request(policy, reason, now)` refuses when the policy is off or the reason is shorter than five characters, and grants with a fixed re-mask moment; `AuditFor` builds the audit event (module orders, action `pii-reveal` / `pii-reveal-denied`, order keys, the field names and the sanitized reason — never a value). `Catalog/PiiRevealPolicyStore.cs` (new): `CatalogStore.GetPiiRevealPolicy` / `SavePiiRevealPolicy` — one row beside the stock and price policies, off by default. `OrdersStore.ReadCustomer` / `SaveCustomer` (new): table `order_customers` keyed like orders; an empty customer deletes the row.

Wired: `PolicyCenterPanel.cs` — a "Kişisel veri" tab (allow toggle, seconds, reason required, save with the clamped value echoed). `OrdersPanel.cs` — `RenderCustomerSection` for a saved order ("order-customer-section", a focusable tab stop): masked field lines by default (or "Kayıtlı müşteri iletişim verisi yok"), a reason box and "Göster (gerekçeli)"; every click goes through `PiiReveal.Request` and is appended to the `AuditStore` before anything is shown; a refusal keeps the mask and names why; a grant re-renders with editable fields (the only way to enter or change customer data), "Müşteri verisini kaydet" (local only) and "Maskele", and a `DispatcherTimer` that re-masks at the policy's moment if the same detail is still open. The revealed values are never written to the status line, the audit or the snapshot.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PiiRevealTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): every mask stub and edge case; refusal without policy, refusal without reason, grant with the clamped re-mask moment, reason optional when the policy says so; the audit names fields and reason (a token in the reason redacted) and no value, denied events marked Warning. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderCustomerStoreTests.cs` (new, 1): round-trip through the customer table, the snapshot payload / `ReadAll` / transfer export / `orders` table all free of the data, empty customer deletes, missing keys refused, policy off by default, save clamps, reload persists. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderCustomerSectionUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel — masked stubs and no value in the section; keyboard-reachable controls; policy off → refused with the reason, a `pii-reveal-denied` audit event without values; policy on (5 s) → "ok" refused for length, a proper reason granted → "Müşteri · AÇIK" with the full e-mail in the editor and a `pii-reveal` audit event naming fields and reason only; after the policy's seconds the section re-masks by itself with no editor left; an order without data says so.

REAL_WORK_COUNT=2; FILES_CHANGED=PiiReveal.cs (new), Catalog/PiiRevealPolicyStore.cs (new), OrdersStore.cs, PolicyCenterPanel.cs, OrdersPanel.cs, tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/PiiRevealTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderCustomerStoreTests.cs (new), tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/OrderCustomerSectionUiTests.cs (new), IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md; TEST_RESULT=547 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (Release; last green 542 + 5 new); artifact SHA-256 cb1e1c9ee43a576c980f188b6fa03cf5a2f15d268984b0c5b90f437db96e3cb2 (publish/win-x64/TrMarketplaceHubDesktop.exe, self-contained win-x64 Release, not committed).

Not done: customer data is entered by the operator only — the Etsy client does not capture buyer name/e-mail/address from receipts (a deliberate choice: no PII is pulled into local storage until the user asks for it); the customer table is plain SQLite like the rest of the order data, not encrypted like credentials; the reveal policy is a single app-wide switch, not per store or per operator (the app has no user/role model); the revealed editors are plain TextBoxes, so the clipboard is not blocked while a reveal is open — the guard is the reveal itself (policy + reason + audit + timeout) and the fact that nothing else surfaces the values; the timer re-masks the open detail only — switching orders already drops it.

## #842 — Channel matrix sticky axes (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-842-channel-matrix-sticky-axes off codex/issue-841-customer-pii-reveal (747361a)

New issue. DESIGN: the "matrix" was a flat list (one row per product × channel × shop) — no product axis, no store axis, nothing to keep in view while scrolling, and every store the service found was shown regardless of what the shell offers.

`ChannelMatrixPivot.cs` (new): `Build(rows, allowedStoreKeys)` pivots the service's flat rows into `ChannelMatrix(Columns, Rows)` — one `ChannelMatrixColumn` per channel/shop (keyed with the dashboard's `DashboardStoreFilter.KeyFor`, header "Etsy\nS1", ordered by channel name then shop) and one `ChannelMatrixRow` per product (SKU, name, cells aligned with the columns, an empty cell where the product has no entry, `Labels` bound by index, `Problems` count); a store outside `allowedStoreKeys` gets no column and its cells are dropped whatever the flat rows carried (null = every store the service built). `ChannelMatrixCell` reads without colour: ✔ yayında, ✖ hata, ○ eşleme yok, ◔ bayat, ⏳ bekliyor, ✎ taslak, ⚠ bağlantı (a connection problem outranks the mapping state), with a `SeverityLevel`.

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — `Create(directory, navigate, allowedStoreKeys)` (the shell passes `() => AllowedStoreKeys()` from `MainWindow.Navigation.cs`); a "channel-matrix" `DataGrid` with `FrozenColumnCount = 2` (SKU and product stay put while the store columns scroll), column headers in the header row (they stay put while rows scroll), row and column virtualization with recycling, `SelectionUnit = Cell` so a single store cell is the keyboard-navigable current cell, 110/220/120-DIP columns; `RenderMatrix(target, matrix)` (public, reused by the tests) rebuilds the columns per pivot and names each row for automation ("Kupa (SKU-A): 2 sorunlu mağaza"); a "Görünüm" combo switches Matris (default) ↔ Liste (the old flat grid); the status line adds "N ürün × M mağaza"; "Seçili kanala git" follows the current matrix cell's store.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixPivotTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): columns by channel then shop, rows by product, labels per state, an empty cell where a product has no entry, header text; an unoffered store has no column and no cells, nothing offered → nothing shown; a 1000 × 20 matrix pivots whole (20 000 cells, every cell present, first/last rows) and every state has its own marker, auth error is Blocking. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixUiTests.cs` (new, 2): a 1000 × 20 pivot rendered in a 900 × 500 window — 22 columns, 2 frozen, 1000 items, fewer than 100 rows realized, DIP widths; after scrolling to the far right and half-way down, the SKU cell's x and the header's y are unchanged while far-right store columns are in view and the row count stays virtualized; a store cell becomes the single current/selected cell and its row carries the automation name. The real panel with two products and two enabled connections but only one offered store: two rows, columns "SKU · Ürün · Etsy\nS1" (eBay E9 absent), Matris the default view, Liste available.
