Not done: DPI is covered by DIP column widths and a 900 × 500 DIP window, not a 200 % render; the matrix keeps the panel's fixed 560-DIP height rather than filling the workspace; a cell's full detail (listing id, last error) is only in the Liste view — the matrix cell shows marker + word (a tooltip/legend is the next issue, #843); the matrix is rebuilt on every filter keystroke (a 1000 × 20 pivot is cheap, but there is no debounce).

## #843 — Channel matrix status legend (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-843-channel-matrix-legend off codex/issue-842-channel-matrix-sticky-axes (f056855)

New issue. DESIGN: the matrix cells had markers (#842) but nothing explained them, the marker table lived inside the cell record, and the one real "unsupported" fact — a channel this build can only plan for locally — was not a state at all.

`ChannelMatrixLegend.cs` (new): the one table of matrix states — SYNCED ✔ yayında, PENDING ⏳ bekliyor, DRAFT ✎ taslak, STALE ◔ bayat, MISSING ○ eşleme yok, ERROR ✖ hata, AUTH_ERROR ⚠ bağlantı, LOCAL_ONLY ⊘ yalnız yerel, NONE · kayıt yok — each with a glyph, a word, a description and a level. `ServiceStatuses` names the six mapping statuses the listing service records, and the tests prove each has its entry. `For(mapping, auth, localOnly)`: a channel without live capability first (its connection status is meaningless), then a dead connection, then the recorded mapping state; an unknown state falls back to "kayıt yok" and is never invented into something else; the two modifier keys are not reachable as mapping statuses. `Counts(matrix)` lists only the states present, in legend order, with counts. `ChannelMatrixCell` now delegates marker, word, label and level to the legend (a cell and the legend cannot disagree) and carries `LocalOnly`, which the pivot takes from the service's own capabilities text (`ChannelListingMatrixService.LocalOnlyCapabilities` / `IsLocalOnly`) — the connection catalogue's fact, not a guess. `ChannelMatrixRow.Descriptions` exposes a description per cell.

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — a legend ("channel-matrix-legend") between the filter bar and the matrix, rebuilt on every refresh: one chip per state on screen ("○ eşleme yok (2)"), each a focusable tab stop with `ToolTipService.ShowsToolTipOnKeyboardFocus`, the description as tooltip and `AutomationProperties.HelpText`, accent and border weight from `SeverityStyle` (system colours under high contrast; the glyph + word carry the meaning regardless); "Gösterilecek hücre yok." when nothing is on screen. Every store cell of the matrix carries its legend description as a tooltip that also opens from keyboard focus. The #842 fixtures used "Yerel plan" as filler capability text — now meaningful — and were moved to a live capability; the empty cell's word became "kayıt yok".

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixLegendTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught that saved-but-untested connections read AUTH_ERROR and that local-only must outrank it): every recorded status has an entry, glyphs and words distinct, descriptions present; precedence local-only > connection > mapping, case-insensitive, unknown → "kayıt yok", modifier keys unreachable as statuses; a cell agrees with the legend; counts list only present states in order (Etsy synced/error, Trendyol local-only whatever its mapping, eBay's cell a connection problem, empties counted), `IsLocalOnly`, a description per cell. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixLegendUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel with an Etsy store (never tested → AUTH_ERROR) and a Trendyol store (local-only in this build) — the legend shows exactly "⚠ bağlantı (2)" and "⊘ yalnız yerel (2)", chips are tab stops with keyboard tooltips and help text, every chip leads with a glyph, the Trendyol cells read "⊘ yalnız yerel" with the capability description, and a store cell carries the description as a keyboard-openable tooltip.

## #844 — Channel matrix readiness filters (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-844-channel-matrix-readiness-filters off codex/issue-843-channel-matrix-legend (7ba9826)

New issue. DESIGN: the matrix could be narrowed by text, channel, shop and one raw status code, but not by the states it actually shows, and never by several at once; nothing said how many products a filter hid, nor that a filter had emptied the screen.

`ChannelMatrixReadinessFilter.cs` (new): `Apply(matrix, activeStates)` → `ChannelMatrixReadinessResult(Matrix, Counts, Active, HiddenRows)` — the legend's states as "any of" row filters (a row stays when any of its cells is in an active state; no active state means every row), columns never dropped, counts always from the unfiltered matrix (a chip says what exists even while another chip hides it), only legend keys honoured (an unknown key is dropped, never invented into a state or an accidentally empty screen), `IsEmpty` / `EmptyText` naming the filters that hid everything; `Toggle` returns a new set. One walk over the cells: 100 000 cells filter in milliseconds (pinned by test).

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — the legend chips (#843) are now `ToggleButton`s that toggle a state in the filter set (still focusable tab stops with keyboard tooltips; the tooltip says whether the filter is on; `AutomationProperties.Name` "hata: 3 hücre, filtre açık"), a "Filtreyi kaldır" button appears while any filter is active, `RefreshGrid` applies the filter to the pivot before rendering and shows "channel-matrix-empty" with the zero-result text when nothing is left; the status line adds "N ürün durum filtresiyle gizli". Counts are recomputed on every text/channel/shop change (a store switch), from that view's whole matrix.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixReadinessFilterTests.cs` (new, 2, red first — the first run caught my own miscount of a fixture): no filter → all rows; one state → its rows with the hidden count and unchanged whole-matrix counts and columns; three states (mixed case) combine as any-of; a state with no cells → empty with the state named and "filtreyi kaldırın"; unknown keys dropped; the empty-matrix text; toggle is pure. 100 000 cells: every cell counted once, filtered under 1.5 s (measured in milliseconds). `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixReadinessFilterUiTests.cs` (new, 1): real panel (Etsy S1 never tested → connection problem, Trendyol T1 local-only) — two off chips that are focusable tab stops; toggling "bağlantı" keeps both rows, reads "filtre açık" and shows the reset; switching the store filter to T1 empties the matrix with the named zero-result text while the local-only chip still counts that store's whole view; adding "yalnız yerel" brings the rows back (any-of); clearing the store filter shows both chips on; the reset clears both. The #843 UI test now finds the chips as toggle buttons.

## #845 — Channel matrix bulk preview drawer (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-845-channel-matrix-bulk-drawer off codex/issue-844-channel-matrix-readiness-filters (dc80ae5)

`ChannelMatrixBulk.cs` (new): the drawer's model. `Revision(rows)` — an order-independent SHA-256 fingerprint (16 hex) of product × store × mapping state × listing × auth per flat row, the "matrix as shown"; `Compose(preview, target, allowedStoreKeys, revisionAtPreview, selectedCount)` — lines re-derived from the bulk preview (READY when Before ≠ After, SKIP "plan zaten aynı" when identical, ERROR with a sanitized reason), affected / skipped / errors counts, the first 50 lines plus `LinesTruncated`, and a `CanApply` with one plain reason: empty selection, target not among the offered stores ("yanlış mağazaya yazılmaz"), no preview, or nothing to change; `CheckBeforeApply(revisionAtPreview, revisionNow, allowedStoreKeys, targetKey, appliedPreviews, previewId)` — the guards that run again at the click: already applied (idempotency), store no longer offered (wrong store), matrix changed since the preview (stale revision).

`ChannelMatrixBulkDrawer.cs` (new): the drawer itself on `DialogShell` (destructive layout: Enter is Vazgeç). A target store combo listing only the pivot's columns (the stores the shell offers), defaulting to the one store the selected cells share; a target category; "Önizle" running `BulkProductOperations.Preview` (`SetChannelMapping` → local `ChannelProductPlan`s only) off the UI thread under a cancellation token — products deleted since the matrix loaded become ERROR lines instead of vanishing; the summary "Hedef: … · N seçili ürün · A uygulanacak · S zaten aynı · E engelli", the reason line, the line list with "… ve N daha", an approval checkbox enabled only when something can be applied, a status line. "Onayla ve uygula" is refused while a preview or apply runs, without a preview, without the box, or when `CheckBeforeApply` says no (the refusal goes to the status line and the box is cleared); otherwise `Apply(preview, approved: true, token, progress)` runs off the UI thread, the preview id is recorded in the owner's ledger, the result is handed back and the window closes. Vazgeç cancels the token (a running preview stops; an apply stops in its validation phase; the commit phase finishes, as `BulkProductOperations` documents) and closes; the X is held only while a write is in flight. No live marketplace write exists on this path.

Wired: `ChannelListingMatrixPanel.cs` — the matrix's cell selection is now Extended (Shift/Ctrl, a whole store column); the "Toplu plan…" command ("channel-matrix-bulk") takes the selected cells' distinct products, the store they share as the default target, the revision of the loaded rows, a fresh-revision function (rebuilt from the stores at the click), the shell's `allowedStoreKeys`, the panel-level applied-preview ledger, and shows the drawer modally; after an apply the status line keeps "Son toplu plan: N yazıldı · S atlandı · E engelli" across the refresh that follows. An empty selection or the Liste view is refused on the error surface before any drawer opens.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixBulkTests.cs` (new, 2, red first): counts, markers and the sanitized error reason; refusals for empty selection, wrong store, no change and no preview; the 1000-line cap (50 shown, 950 more); the revision ignores order and moves on a state/listing change or a lost row; apply refused when stale, wrong-store or repeated, allowed for a fresh preview. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ChannelMatrixBulkUiTests.cs` (new, 1): the real panel with 60 products × 2 stores — an empty selection is refused on the error surface with no owned window; the whole Etsy column selected → the modal drawer (driven from inside ShowDialog's nested loop) defaults to Etsy · S1, previews "60 uygulanacak", lists 50 with "… ve 10 daha", refuses apply without the box, then writes 60 local plans (category, no listing id) to Etsy only and the panel refreshes with the result note; the same selection again previews "60 zaten aynı", refuses, and Cancel closes without writing; on a drawer built for three products: a store removed from the offered set after the preview, a changed revision, and an already applied preview id each refuse at the click with nothing written; Cancel closes with nothing written; a clean apply writes three, closes, reports and records the preview. The UI test caught one defect before commit: the drawer's own Closing guard held the window open after a successful apply.

Not done: one bulk operation only — create/update a local plan with a target category; "clear plan" needs a delete on `ChannelProductsStore` and a kind on `BulkProductOperations`, neither of which exists; a category-only plan leaves the cell MISSING because the matrix keys mapping on the listing id (a "plan var, ilan yok" state would be a matrix-semantics change across #842–#844); the applied-preview ledger is per panel instance and in memory (the plan upsert is itself idempotent, so a repeat after a restart re-saves identical rows rather than duplicating); the drawer does not re-run the preview on category edits, only on "Önizle" and on a target change.

## #846 — Report catalog card layout (2026-09-13), branch codex/issue-846-report-catalog-cards off codex/issue-845-channel-matrix-bulk-drawer (e71fb74)

`ReportCatalog.cs` (new): the catalog is a fixed table of the reports this build really produces — nine definitions (product Excel and XML exports, rejected import rows, product order summary, orders list, listing matrix, data quality, API health, support package), each with its purpose, the local data it reads, Global/Store scope, output kinds and the owner route. `Describe` turns one into a card: title trimmed at 60 characters for the card and kept whole for the tooltip and the accessible name; "Kapsam: tüm veri · …" or "Kapsam: N sunulan mağaza · …"; the last run as "Son çalıştırma: 3 dk önce · başarılı · 1.234 satır" (failed/cancelled without a row count) or "Hiç çalıştırılmadı"; the saved-filter count; "Çıktı: Excel, CSV"; hidden when store-scoped and the shell offers no store. `Build` gives cards + hidden + total with a search over title/purpose/sources and one empty text per cause (no reports / store-scoped hidden / search); `Load` reads the real run store and the real saved views (`UiPreferenceStore` module "report:<key>"). `ReportRunStore` (new, `report_runs.db`): `Record(key, started, state, rows, note?, store?)` validates the key (lowercase letters/digits/dash), refuses `Never`, clamps rows, keeps only a file name from a note and sanitizes it; `Latest()` is the newest run per key; `Recent(key, n)`.

`ReportsPanel.cs` (new): the "Raporlar" workspace — heading, search ("report-search"), "Yenile", the count line ("report-count": "9 rapor · 4 mağaza kapsamlı rapor gizli (sunulan mağaza yok) · N aramaya uymadı"), the empty text ("report-empty") and the card grid ("report-cards"): one 300-DIP focusable `Button` per card (title / purpose / scope / last run with the severity glyph and accent / saved filters / outputs; tooltip and `AutomationProperties.Name` carry the whole title and the run; the tooltip opens from the keyboard); Enter or click opens the owner route. `Render(host, view, open)` is the public layout step.

Wired: `MainWindow.Navigation.cs` registers the `reports` route (YÖNETİM, before diagnostics) with the shell's `AllowedStoreKeys` — the screen-parity audit that logged "Ekran paritesi BLOCKED: reports" at every start is now complete, the dashboard's "Raporlar" quick card (gated by `routeExists` since #813) appears, and the help topic "reports" describes the page that now exists. Real runs feed the cards: the product Excel export (`MainWindow.Excel.cs`), the rejected-rows export (Succeeded / Cancelled / Failed with the written count, including the cancellation path), the product XML export (`MainWindow.xaml.cs`) and the support package (`DiagnosticsPanel.cs`) each record a run.

`tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportCatalogTests.cs` (new, 3, red first): card texts for never / succeeded / failed / cancelled runs, scope by offered stores, filters and outputs, an 80-character title trimmed to 60 with the whole title in the accessible name; zero definitions; fifty (25 store-scoped hidden without a store, the empty text naming the cause); search including a zero result; runs and filters for keys outside the catalog ignored; catalog keys unique and every title within the limit; the run store keeps the latest per key, keeps a file name and drops a token/password from a note, refuses bad keys and `Never`. `tests/MarketplaceHub.Tests/ReportsPanelUiTests.cs` (new, 2): the real panel — nine focusable, named, 300-DIP cards all "Hiç çalıştırılmadı"; a card takes keyboard focus; a recorded run and a saved view change the cards on Yenile; with no offered store the four store-scoped cards go and the count says so; search narrows to the support package, names a zero result and restores; a card navigates to its owner (diagnostics); fifty rendered cards wrap into rows at 300 DIP with the trimmed title on the card and the whole one in the tooltip. The real shell: `routes` contains "reports", `ScreenParityAudit` is complete, Navigate reaches it, and the cards follow the shell's own store list.
